//! Concrete question evidence; this never changes gate or invariant verdicts.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#19-wave-28-output-and-witness-decisions-dx11 — inferred witnesses never become authored refutations.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::{Diagnostic,security::challenges::{self,Counterexample}};
use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String {use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
// Generated values only: retain typed scalars, redact opaque payloads.
fn safe(value:&snapback4_core::Value,kind:Option<&ColumnType>)->serde_json::Value {
    use snapback4_core::Value;
    match (value,kind.map(ColumnType::required)) {
        (_,Some(ColumnType::Text{..}|ColumnType::Json{..}|ColumnType::Bytes{..}|ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}))=>json!("<redacted>"),
        (Value::Array(xs),Some(ColumnType::List{of,..}|ColumnType::ListRange{of,..}))=>json!(xs.iter().map(|v|safe(v,Some(of))).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
        (Value::String(_)|Value::Bytes(_)|Value::Row(_)|Value::Map(_)|Value::Array(_),None)=>json!("<redacted>"),
        _=>serde_json::to_value(value).unwrap(),
    }
}
fn image(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&snapback4_core::Value)->serde_json::Value {
    let snapback4_core::Value::Row(fields)=row else{return json!("<redacted>");};
    json!(fields.iter().map(|(name,value)|(name,safe(value,bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(name))))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>())
}
fn evidence(bound:&Bound,inv:&security::Invariant,c:&Counterexample)->serde_json::Value {
    let args=c.args.iter().map(|(name,value)|(name,safe(value,None))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    let old=c.old.as_ref().map(|row|image(bound,&inv.table,row));let next=image(bound,&inv.table,&c.row);
    let summary=format!("{}({}) as {} → ok; {} {} -> {}; candidate invariant {} only {} (finite generated-fixture replay, not proof)",c.operation,serde_json::to_string(&args).unwrap(),c.viewer,inv.target_label(),old.as_ref().map(|v|v.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(||"absent".into()),next,inv.target,inv.predicate);
    let history=c.history.iter().map(|step|json!({"operation":step.operation,"viewer":step.viewer,"args":step.args.iter().map(|(k,v)|(k,safe(v,None))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),"state":step.result["state"]})).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let supporting=c.supporting_rows.iter().map(|(table,rows)|(table,rows.iter().map(|r|image(bound,table,r)).collect::<Vec<_>>())).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    json!({"history":history,"supportingRows":supporting,"summary":summary,"candidate":format!("invariant {} only {}",inv.target_label(),inv.predicate),"operation":c.operation,"viewer":c.viewer,"args":args,"effect":c.effect,"old":old,"row":next,"reachability":c.reachability,"scope":c.scope})
}
fn candidate(sources:&[(String,String)],line:&str)->Option<security::Invariant> {
    let mut sources=sources.to_vec();sources.push(("$dx11-question.q".into(),format!("{line}\n")));
    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return None;}
    let mut inv=bound.invariants.into_iter().find(|i|i.site.file=="$dx11-question.q")?;
    inv.site.file=format!("$derived/dx11/{}",hash(line.as_bytes()));Some(inv)
}
fn candidates(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[Diagnostic])->Vec<(usize,security::Invariant)> {
    let derived=security::derived::invariants(sources,bound);let mut out=Vec::new();
    for (index,n) in notes.iter().enumerate() {
        if n.code=="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY" {for inv in &derived {if inv.site==n.site {out.push((index,inv.clone()));}}}
        if n.code=="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" {
            if let Some(subject)=n.message.split(": ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split_whitespace().next()) {
                for inv in &derived {if subject==format!("{}.{}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or("")) {out.push((index,inv.clone()));}}
                if let Some(policy)=n.message.split("without the edit restriction of ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split_once(": ").map(|(_,p)|p)).and_then(|s|s.split("; creation admission:").next()) {
                    if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {subject} update only {policy}")) {out.push((index,inv));}
                }
            }
        }
        if n.code=="N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" {
            let Some(subject)=n.message.split("reader-grant column ").nth(1) else{continue;};let Some((table,column))=subject.split_once('.') else{continue;};
            // Name-matched role membership is a candidate question, never a rule.
            for (roles,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables() {for (role,kind) in &t.columns {
                if let ColumnType::Enum{variants}=kind.required() {if variants.iter().any(|v|v==column) {
                    if let Some(principal)=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Principal).then_some(c)) {
                        if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {table} always exists {roles}[{principal} = .{column}, {role} = '{column}']")) {out.push((index,inv));}
                    }
                }}
            }}
        }
        if n.code=="N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" {
            let Some((subject,_))=n.message.split_once(':') else{continue;};let Some((table,column))=subject.split_once('.') else{continue;};let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table) else{continue;};
            if !matches!(t.columns.get(column).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Bool)){continue;}
            let stem=column.to_lowercase().split("approv").next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
            let trigger=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|(c!=column&&!stem.is_empty()&&c.to_lowercase().starts_with(&stem)&&matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Bool)).then_some(c));
            let predicate=trigger.map(|trigger|format!("not .{trigger} or .{column}")).unwrap_or_else(||format!(".{column}"));
            let Some(ops)=n.message.split("transitions ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split(" do not").next()) else{continue;};
            // A column-change candidate uses the campaign's mature transition
            // exploration; it observes the same operation, without adding a rule.
            fn columns(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,out:&mut std::collections::BTreeSet<String>) {
                if let Some(write)=value.get("WriteTarget").filter(|w|w["target"]["table"]==table) {
                    if let Some(fields)=write["patch"]["Object"].as_object(){out.extend(fields.keys().cloned());}
                }
                if let Some(write)=value.get("Update").filter(|w|w["table"]==table) {
                    if let Some(fields)=write["patch"]["Object"].as_object(){out.extend(fields.keys().cloned());}
                }
                match value {serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){columns(v,table,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{columns(v,table,out)},_=>{}}
            }
            for op in ops.split(", ").collect::<Vec<_>>().into_iter().rev() {
                if let Some(program)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==op) {
                    let mut changed=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();columns(&serde_json::to_value(&program.program).unwrap(),table,&mut changed);
                    for col in changed.into_iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(c).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Time))) {
                        if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {table}.{col} update only {predicate}")) {out.push((index,inv));}
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }out
}
/// Once an edit is admitted, try a concrete decreasing edit through that same
/// operation. Both writes run on the engine; no fabricated old/new values.
fn decreasing_edit(bound:&Bound,inv:&security::Invariant,c:&Counterexample)->Option<Counterexample> {
    use snapback4_core::{Engine,Context,Value};
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;
    if !matches!(bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?.required(),ColumnType::Money{..}|ColumnType::Int|ColumnType::IntRange{..}|ColumnType::Decimal{..}) || !c.args.contains_key(column) {return None;}
    let mut engine=Engine::memory(bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).ok()?;
    let mut dependencies=inv.policy.as_ref().map(security::kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();dependencies.insert(inv.table.clone());
    let id=c.old.as_ref()?.field("id").ok()?.clone();
    let mut rows=c.supporting_rows.iter().filter(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(*table)).flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.iter().map(move |row|(table.clone(),row.clone()))).filter(|(table,row)|table!=&inv.table||row.field("id").ok()!=Some(&id)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    rows.push((inv.table.clone(),c.old.clone()?));engine.seed_at(rows,challenges::NOW).ok()?;
    let mut high=c.args.clone();high.insert(column.clone(),Value::Int(54321));
    let first=engine.mutate(&c.operation,None,Context::new(&c.viewer,high.clone()).at(challenges::NOW)).ok()?;
    if serde_json::to_value(&first).ok()?["state"]!="sent"{return None;}
    let old=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).ok()?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(&id))?;
    let mut low=c.args.clone();low.insert(column.clone(),Value::Int(0));
    let second=engine.mutate(&c.operation,None,Context::new(&c.viewer,low.clone()).at(challenges::NOW)).ok()?;
    if serde_json::to_value(&second).ok()?["state"]!="sent"{return None;}
    let row=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).ok()?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(&id))?;
    if !matches!((old.field(column).ok()?,row.field(column).ok()?),(Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b)) if a>b){return None;}
    let mut next=c.clone();next.args=low.clone();next.old=Some(old);next.row=row;
    if let Some(step)=c.history.last() {let mut first_step=step.clone();first_step.operation=c.operation.clone();first_step.viewer=c.viewer.clone();first_step.args=high;first_step.result=serde_json::to_value(first).ok()?;next.history.push(first_step);let mut second_step=step.clone();second_step.operation=c.operation.clone();second_step.viewer=c.viewer.clone();second_step.args=low;second_step.result=serde_json::to_value(second).ok()?;next.history.push(second_step);}
    Some(next)
}
/// Fixed work cap; generated cache never clears a gate or supplies authority.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn collect(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[Diagnostic])->Vec<Option<serde_json::Value>> {
    let key=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&("dx11-witness-4",sources,notes)).unwrap());let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-witnesses.json");
    if let Some(cached)=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&b).ok()) {if cached["key"]==key {if let Ok(values)=serde_json::from_value(cached["values"].clone()){return values;}}}
    let selected=candidates(bound,sources,notes);let mut result=vec![None;notes.len()];
    if !selected.is_empty() {
        let mut questions=bound.clone();questions.invariants=selected.iter().map(|(_,i)|i.clone()).collect();
        let mut campaign=challenges::Campaign{work_remaining:Some(8192),..Default::default()};challenges::generated_campaign(&questions,&mut campaign,false);
        for (index,inv) in &selected {
            if result[*index].is_some(){continue;}
            if let Some(c)=campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable()).max_by_key(|c| {
                let decrease=inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|column|Some((c.old.as_ref()?.field(column).ok()?,c.row.field(column).ok()?))).is_some_and(|(old,next)|matches!((old,next),(snapback4_core::Value::Int(a),snapback4_core::Value::Int(b)) if a>b));
                (decrease,c.history.len())
            }) {let replay=decreasing_edit(bound,inv,c);result[*index]=Some(evidence(bound,inv,replay.as_ref().unwrap_or(c)));}
        }
    }
    let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&json!({"key":key,"values":result})).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);result
}

//! Wave-28 decision commands, concise success and witness records.
use std::{fs,path::PathBuf,process::Command};
use serde_json::Value;
struct Scratch(PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn scratch()->Scratch{static NEXT:std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize=std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx11-{}-{}",std::process::id(),NEXT.fetch_add(1,std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)));fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();Scratch(root)}
fn run(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->std::process::Output{Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4")).current_dir(&p.0).args(args).output().unwrap()}
fn json(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->Value{let out=run(p,&[&["check","--json"][..],args].concat());serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|_|panic!("{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)))}
const SOURCE:&str="table docs:\n  owner:principal\n  body:text <=100\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(body:text <=100):\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,body}\nquery unrelated():\n  return true\n";
#[test]fn passing_check_hides_advisory_details_but_keeps_enforcement_and_next_command(){
 let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),SOURCE).unwrap();
 let out=run(&p,&["check"]);assert!(out.status.success());let text=format!("{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));assert!(text.lines().count()<=5,"{text}");assert!(text.contains("check --review")&&text.contains("enforced by the language"),"{text}");assert!(!text.contains("decision N_")&&!text.contains("is this intended?"),"{text}");
 let expanded=run(&p,&["check","--review"]);let text=String::from_utf8_lossy(&expanded.stdout);assert!(expanded.status.success());assert!(text.contains("Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security"),"{text}");
}
#[test]fn every_json_id_accepts_atomic_repeated_and_comma_syntax_and_unrelated_edits(){
 let p=scratch();let source=p.0.join("snapback/schema.q");fs::write(&source,SOURCE).unwrap();let first=json(&p,&[]);let ids=first["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|d|d["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert!(ids.len()>=2);
 let out=run(&p,&["check","--ack-security",&ids[0],"--ack-security","unknown","--reason","explicit specification rationale"]);assert_eq!(out.status.code(),Some(2));assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("stale"));
 let comma=ids[1..].join(",");let out=run(&p,&["check","--ack-security",&ids[0],"--ack-security",&comma,"--reason","explicit specification rationale"]);assert!(out.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
 fs::write(&source,SOURCE.replace("return true","return false")).unwrap();let after=json(&p,&[]);assert!(after["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().all(|d|d["acknowledged"]==true),"{after}");
}
#[test]fn wave28_false_premise_decisions_retain_the_concrete_witness(){
 for (source,codes) in [(include_str!("fixtures/dx11/011.q"),vec!["N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"]),(include_str!("fixtures/dx11/019.q"),vec!["N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"])] {
  let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();let report=json(&p,&[]);
  for code in codes {
   let decision=report["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|d|d["code"]==code&&d["witness"].is_object()&&(code!="N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"||d["witness"]["row"]["homeowner"].is_object())).unwrap_or_else(||panic!("missing witness for {code}"));
   let witness=&decision["witness"];
   if code=="N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" {assert_eq!(witness["operation"],"recordInstallation");assert_eq!(witness["row"]["indoorMount"]["Bool"],true);assert_eq!(witness["row"]["indoorApproved"]["Bool"],false);}
   if code=="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" {assert_eq!(witness["operation"],"setAccount");assert_eq!(witness["effect"],"update");assert!(witness["old"]["balance"]["Int"].as_i64().unwrap()>witness["row"]["balance"]["Int"].as_i64().unwrap());}
   let id=decision["id"].as_str().unwrap();let result=json(&p,&["--ack-security",id,"--reason","The specification explicitly permits this witnessed behavior"]);assert_eq!(result["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|d|d["id"]==id).unwrap()["acknowledged"],true);
   let recorded:Value=serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(recorded["entries"][id]["witness"],*witness);
  }
 }
}

 let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),"use personas alice, bob\ntable docs:\n  owner:principal\n  body:text <=100 write <- .owner = viewer\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(owner:principal,body:text <=100):\n  return insert docs {owner,body}\n").unwrap();

 assert!(!out.status.success());let public=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);let private=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
 assert!(public.contains("E_RULE"),"{public} {private}");assert!(!public.contains("column write grant")&&!public.contains("write <-"),"{public}");assert!(private.contains("column write grant body")&&private.contains("body:text <=100 write <-"),"{private}");
}

 let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),format!("{SOURCE}\ninvariant docs.read only .owner = viewer\n")).unwrap();let report=json(&p,&[]);let id=report["securityReviews"][0]["id"].as_str().unwrap();
 let out=run(&p,&["check","--ack-security",id,"--reason","An advisory cannot clear an authored refutation"]);assert_eq!(out.status.code(),Some(1));assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("FIX"));
}
//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — full audit is opt-in, intent notes are on ordinary check.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
mod record;
mod presence;
pub(super) mod witnesses;
#[cfg(test)] mod derived_tests;
pub(super) use presence::remember as remember_presence;
pub(super) use record::{remember,capture};
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(project,bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->Report{
    let augmented=if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){security::derived::augmented(sources,bound)}else{bound.clone()};
    let bound=&augmented;
    let mut selection=options.clone();
    let selected_fixture=record::selected_fixture(project,options);
    if selected_fixture.is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),selected_fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let inputs=match selection.flags.get("fixture"){
        Some(path)=>match super::test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{let mut report=security::analyze_rules(bound);security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);report.campaign_coverage.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));return report;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&inputs{selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let cached=record::matching(project,&selection,sources);
    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){let mut campaign=security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(10000);campaign.work_remaining=Some(8192);campaign}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)}};
    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    // An explicitly selected fixture remains the initial-state assumption.
    // Static generation runs only on the fixture-free path; both share this
    // one bounded campaign with all subsequent concrete exploration.
    let mut report=if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")||known_failure||options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){
        security::analyze_rules(bound)
    }else{security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign)};
    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{
                    // An empty starter directory selects generation, just as
                    // no fixture does. Give the direct contradiction its turn
                    // before empty-start mutation permutations spend the cap.
                    report=security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign);
                    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
                    security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)
                },
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,None,sources).unwrap_or_else(|why|vec![snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING",why,"repair the unreadable generated presence baseline").guide("security")]));
    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    let mut canonical=security::analyze_rules(bound);
    security::add_source_notes(&mut canonical,sources,bound);
    canonical.notes.extend(report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING").cloned());
    report.notes=canonical.notes;
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let sources=match project.sources(){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}};
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(snapback4_lang::compile(&sources),options.json);
    // Audit is a permission map plus bounded evidence; `test` owns full campaigns.
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&sources,options);
    let dispositions=match super::security_reviews::review(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None){Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{eprintln!("audit: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");return 2;}};
    if options.json {let mut value=serde_json::to_value(&report).unwrap();value["securityReviews"]=serde_json::to_value(&dispositions).unwrap();println!("{value}");}
    else {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);print!("{}",human.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));super::security_reviews::print(&dispositions,&report.notes);}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — report enforcement facts without claiming complete security.
pub(super) fn enforced(report:&Report,bound:&Bound){
    let roles=report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").count();
    let columns=bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(_,t)|t.column_reads.len()).sum::<usize>();
    let uploads=bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|t.columns.values()).filter(|t|t.contains_asset()).count();
    println!("check: enforced by the language — {uploads} typed upload columns with owner-bound placement; {columns} column audiences; {roles} possible self-assignable role paths detected (bounded analysis)");
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let failures=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted).count();
    let rule_enforced=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&v.direct_rule==security::Status::Proved).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted; {rule_enforced} enforced by rule (additional paths not proved); {} other unproved ({} failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted-rule_enforced,failures.len());
    for v in &failures {
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        eprintln!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap());
    }
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    if supplemental>0 {eprintln!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)");}
    if verbose {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);eprint!("{}",human.text_with_all(true));}
    else if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (check --verbose/JSON keeps every reason)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  review audiences and {} uncovered exposures: snapback4 audit",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options)->Result<Option<String>,String>{
    let changed=match prior {
        None=>true,
        Some(old)=>serde_json::to_vec(&old.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?,
    };
    if !changed{return Ok(None);}
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#153-rule-changing-deployment-review — review is prominent, strictness is explicit and failure-origin aware.
    let started=std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut preflight=options.clone();if !options.flags.contains_key("staging")||options.flags.contains_key("strict"){preflight.flags.insert("security-deploy".into(),String::new());}
    let mut report=report(project,bound,sources,&preflight);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,prior,sources)?);
    if super::proof_source::sources(project,options,"schema")?!=sources{return Err("selected Quarry sources changed during deployment security preflight".into());}
    let dispositions=super::security_reviews::review_snapshot(project,sources,&report.notes)?;
    let derived=security::derived::notes(sources,bound);
    let acknowledged=derived.iter().filter(|n|report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).any(|(r,d)|r.site==n.site&&r.code==n.code&&r.message==n.message&&d.acknowledged)).map(|n|n.site.to_string()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
    let pending=super::security_reviews::pending(&dispositions);
    let fixes=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())&&(!security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)||!acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()))).count();
    eprintln!("security deploy campaign: {} ms; bounded replay, remaining paths retain coverage gaps",started.elapsed().as_millis());
    let scope=release_scope(bound,prior,sources);
    let mut notice=String::from("SECURITY REVIEW — rules changed; changed tables and operations only\n");
    for v in report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())){
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        if security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)&&acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()){
            notice.push_str(&format!("  REVIEWED derived {}: individual specification decision retained\n",v.invariant.target_label()));continue;
        }
        notice.push_str(&format!("  FIX {} {}: refuted via {} as {} ({:?}); repair enforcement to match intent, then run snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,c.reachability));
        if !security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant) {
            if v.invariant.target=="write"&&v.invariant.column.is_some()&&c.effect=="insert" {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if broader creation is deliberate, replace this assertion with `invariant {}.{} update only {}`; then run snapback4 test. Authored contradictions cannot be cleared with --ack-security\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,v.invariant.column.as_ref().unwrap(),v.invariant.predicate));
            }else {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if this observed audience is deliberate, revise the authored assertion here to match that specification, then run snapback4 test; --ack-security applies only to derived questions, never an authored contradiction\n",v.invariant.site));
            }
        }
        let aggregates=bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.source==v.invariant.table).map(|m|m.target.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if c.observations.is_some()&&!aggregates.is_empty()&&v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            notice.push_str(&format!("  DERIVED {}: a separately readable aggregate changes in the paired observation; tightening the source read rule alone cannot clear this disclosure\n",aggregates.join(", ")));
            let field=v.invariant.predicate.split('.').skip(1).filter_map(|part|part.split(|c:char|!c.is_alphanumeric()&&c!='_').next()).find(|field|bound.schema.table(&v.invariant.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(*field)).is_some_and(|kind|matches!(kind.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)));
            if let Some(field)=field {notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if aggregate counts are intentionally public, replace the whole-row assertion with `invariant {}.{} read only {}` for this protected field and independently declare any other private fields; otherwise restrict the aggregate audience; rerun snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,field,v.invariant.predicate));}
        }else if v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            for (file,text) in sources {
                let doc=snapback4_lang::parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
                if let Some(table)=doc.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==v.invariant.table) {
                    if let Some(rule)=table.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==snapback4_lang::ast::Effect::Read) {
                        let expanded=bound.refusal_sites.get(&(v.invariant.table.clone(),"$read-rule".into()));
                        if expanded.is_some() {
                            notice.push_str(&format!("  SOURCE {}: preserve `{}` and its parent lifetime/authority; if this audience is unintended, repair the parent read rule before rerunning snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.table,snapback4_lang::bind::spell_internals(&snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value))));
                        }else {
                            notice.push_str(&format!("  SOURCE {}: retain the current audience and enforce this declared intent with `read <- ({}) and ({})`; rerun snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.table,snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value),v.invariant.predicate));
                        }
                        let predicate=expanded.cloned().unwrap_or_else(||snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value));
                        notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if every reader admitted by the current read rule is deliberately allowed, replace this assertion with `invariant {}.read only {predicate}`; then run snapback4 test and deploy again. This is an authored specification change, not --ack-security\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

    }
    let mut notes=report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code!="N_SECURITY_INTENT"&&scope.iter().any(|(file,start,end)|n.site.file==*file&&n.site.line>=*start&&n.site.line<*end)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    notes.sort_by_key(|n|crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code));
    let count=notes.len();
    // Never suppress a gate or write-authority question behind the display cap.
    for (i,n) in notes.into_iter().enumerate(){if crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review&&(i<5||security::note_priority(&n.code)<=3){notice.push_str(&format!("  REVIEW {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",n.site,n.code,n.message,n.rewrite));}}
    if count>5 {notice.push_str(&format!("  {count} changed-scope notes in deploy --check --json\n"));}
    let selected=report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).filter(|(n,d)|d.gated || n.code!="N_SECURITY_INTENT" && scope.iter().any(|(file,start,end)| n.site.file==*file&&n.site.line>=*start&&n.site.line<*end)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let complete_family=dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.family.is_some()).count()==selected.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()).count();
    let scoped_notes=selected.iter().map(|(n,_)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let scoped_dispositions=selected.iter().map(|(_,d)| {let mut d=(*d).clone(); if !complete_family {d.family=None;} d}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // A scoped deploy cannot silently acknowledge unseen family members. It
    // prints their individual commands; ordinary check offers the whole family.
    notice.push_str(&super::security_reviews::render(&scoped_dispositions,&scoped_notes));
    let test=release_notice(project,bound,sources,options);
    notice.push_str(&format!("  security exposure: {} tables; {} independent invariants; {} coverage gaps\n  {}",report.exposures.len(),bound.invariants.len(),report.campaign_coverage.len(),test));
    let verdict=format!("security FIX {fixes}; {} pending review; {test}",dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged).count());
    Ok(Some(serde_json::json!({"notice":notice,"verdict":verdict,"fixes":fixes,"pending":pending,"changedNotes":count,"report":report,"securityReviews":dispositions}).to_string()))
}
fn age(timestamp:Option<i64>,now:i64)->String{
    timestamp.map(|t|format!("at {t} ms since Unix epoch, age {}s",now.saturating_sub(t).max(0)/1000)).unwrap_or_else(||"time/age unknown (legacy record)".into())
}
fn release_notice(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->String{
    let selection=options.flags.get("schema").map(|s|format!(" --source {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let fixture=options.flags.get("fixture").map(|s|format!(" --fixture {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let now=snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let matching=record::matching(project,options,sources);
    let matches=matching.is_some();
    match matching.or_else(||record::latest(project)){
        Some(record)=>{
            let result=if !record.complete{"incomplete"}else if record.run_violations.unwrap_or(record.violations.len())>0{"FAILED"}else{"completed, no observed violation"};
            let current=if matches{"matches selected source/fixtures/CLI"}else{"STALE: source, fixtures or CLI differ; does not qualify selected release"};
            let next=if matches&&record.complete{"matching test campaign recorded (finite, not proof)".into()}else{format!("run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment")};
            format!("last test: {result}, {}; {current}; {} retained reachable refutations, {} coverage gaps; last completed test {}; {next}",age(record.recorded_at,now),record.violations.len(),record.coverage_gaps,age(record.last_completed_at,now))
        },
        None=>format!("last test: NEVER RECORDED; run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment; no matching completed campaign for this source, fixtures and CLI; {} independent invariants",bound.invariants.len()),
    }
}

// SEC7 owns ordinary check ranking/gates; this scope and output belong to ROLL.
fn release_scope(bound:&Bound,prior:Option<&Bound>,sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,u32,u32)> {
    let mut scope=Vec::new();
    fn normalized(p:&impl serde::Serialize)->serde_json::Value {
        fn clear(v:&mut serde_json::Value){match v{serde_json::Value::Object(o)=>{o.remove("site");for v in o.values_mut(){clear(v);}},serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{clear(v)},_=>()}}
        let mut v=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();clear(&mut v);v
    }
    for(file,text)in sources {
        let doc=snapback4_lang::parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
        let mut starts=text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_,s)|!s.is_empty()&&!s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace)&&!s.starts_with("--")).map(|(i,_)|i as u32+1).collect::<Vec<_>>();starts.sort();
        let mut add=|line:u32|scope.push((file.clone(),line,starts.iter().copied().find(|n|*n>line).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)));
        for t in &doc.tables {if prior.is_none_or(|old|old.schema.tables.get(&t.name).map(normalized)!=bound.schema.tables.get(&t.name).map(normalized)){add(t.site.line);}}
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let next=bound.programs.iter().find(|b|b.program.name==p.name);
            let old=prior.and_then(|b|b.programs.iter().find(|b|b.program.name==p.name));
            if next.map(|b|normalized(&b.program))!=old.map(|b|normalized(&b.program)){add(p.site.line);}
        }
    }scope
}
pub(super) fn print_release(summary:Option<&String>){if let Some(s)=summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()){eprintln!("{}",s["notice"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}}
pub(super) fn release_verdict(summary:Option<&String>)->String {summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()).and_then(|s|s["verdict"].as_str().map(str::to_owned)).unwrap_or_else(||"security unchanged".into())}

pub(super) fn enforce_release(summary:Option<&String>,options:&Options)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(value)=summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok())else{return Ok(())};
    if !options.flags.contains_key("staging")||options.flags.contains_key("strict"){
        if value["fixes"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0)>0{return Err("E_SECURITY_REVIEW: production deploy refuses reachable FIX; repair the named rule and rerun snapback4 test; for a derived candidate only, run check --ack-security ID --reason '<specification rationale>' using the reported rule ID".into())}
        if value["pending"].as_bool()==Some(true){return Err("E_SECURITY_REVIEW: production deploy requires individual security review; run each reported check --ack-security ID --reason '<specification decision>', or enforce the named intent in source".into())}
    }Ok(())
}

+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/run_refusal.rs
@@ -11,7 +11,9 @@ use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
 
 /// A local run's trace, in the shape of a dev refusal record.
 pub(super) fn local(trace: &RefusalTrace, sites: &BTreeMap<(String, String), String>) -> Option<Json> {
-    trace.take().filter(|cause| cause.terminal).map(|cause| record(&cause, sites))
+    trace.take().filter(|cause| cause.terminal || trace.hidden()>0).map(|cause| {
+        let mut value=record(&cause, sites);value["hiddenRows"]=json!(trace.hidden());value
+    })
 }
 
 /// The refusal's own authored site takes precedence over any earlier trace.
@@ -21,7 +23,7 @@ pub(super) fn for_refusal(trace: &RefusalTrace, sites: &BTreeMap<(String, String
         trace.take();
         let kind = if kind == "program require" { "mutation require" } else { kind.as_str() };
         return Some(json!({"table":"", "clause":kind, "phase":kind, "site":site, "siteKind":"program",
-            "ruleText":sites.get(&("$clause".into(), site.clone()))}));
+            "hiddenRows":trace.hidden(), "ruleText":sites.get(&("$clause".into(), site.clone()))}));
     }
     local(trace, sites)
 }
@@ -144,6 +146,7 @@ pub(super) fn explain(record: &Json, source: &dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) ->
         (None, Some(site), Some(line)) => format!("{clause}, at `{line}` ({site})"),
         (_, site, _) => format!("{clause} ({}{generation})", site.unwrap_or("<generated>")),
     };
+    let text = if record["hiddenRows"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0)>0 {format!("{text}; rows were hidden by a read rule; program lookups/exists see only caller-visible rows, and hidden columns are omitted") } else {text};
     let text = if let Some(phase) = record["phase"].as_str().filter(|phase| phase.starts_with("table authorization layer:")) {
         format!("{text}; {phase}")
     } else { text };
diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/assets.ts b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/assets.ts
index 5f95b2b59a..adb53c5f5f 100644
--- a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/assets.ts
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/assets.ts
@@ -89,7 +89,8 @@ export async function uploadAsset(base: string, headers: Record<string, string>,
       try { json = await response.json(); }
       catch { return { ok: false, why: invalidResponse(response.status, "upload") }; }
       if (response.ok && json?.asset) return { ok: true, asset: json.asset };
-      const why = refusal(json?.denied) ?? invalidResponse(response.status, "upload");
+      const denied = refusal(json?.denied) ?? invalidResponse(response.status, "upload");
+      const why = denied.code === "E_RATE_LIMIT" ? { ...denied, message: `${denied.message}; upload admission is busy: send files sequentially, then retry the same selected bytes after Retry-After (automatic retries are bounded)` } : denied;
       // Only an explicit admission refusal proves these bytes were not stored.
       // A transport failure or another retryable code may follow acceptance.
       if (why.code !== "E_RATE_LIMIT" || why.retryable !== true || attempt === 3 || !canRetry()) return { ok: false, why };
diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/react.tsx b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/react.tsx
index 0d9ca02ac3..607b721ed1 100644
--- a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/react.tsx
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/src/react.tsx
@@ -275,6 +275,10 @@ export type UseQuery<O> = O extends Query<any, any> ? Read<ResultOf<O>> : never;
  * Guards take `page.read`; `matchRead` also accepts the whole page. */
 export interface PageHook<R extends readonly unknown[]> {
   items: R[number][];
+  /** True only while the combined read is loading. Partial/stale rows remain data. */
+  loading: boolean;
+  /** The combined read's refusal, or null. Completeness/freshness stay on read. */
+  why: Refusal | null;
   hasMore: boolean;
   read: Read<R>;
   loadMore: () => void;
@@ -300,7 +304,7 @@ export function usePage<A, R extends readonly unknown[]>(op: PagedQuery<A, R>, a
   const paged = op as Query<A & { c?: Cursor | null }, R>;
   const pages = useMemo(() => skip ? SKIPPED_PAGES : new Pages(client, paged, args as A & { c?: Cursor | null }, observeOptions(options)), [client, op, key, options?.keep, skip]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(pages.subscribe, pages.getSnapshot, pages.getSnapshot);
-  return { items: snapshot.items as R[number][], hasMore: snapshot.hasMore, read: snapshot.read as Read<R>, loadMore: pages.loadMore };
+  return { loading: "loading" in snapshot.read, why: "denied" in snapshot.read ? snapshot.read.denied : null, items: snapshot.items as R[number][], hasMore: snapshot.hasMore, read: snapshot.read as Read<R>, loadMore: pages.loadMore };
 }
 /** The page chain a skipped `usePage` holds: loading, no items, nothing to load. */
 const SKIPPED_PAGE_SNAPSHOT = { items: [] as never[], hasMore: false, read: LOADING };
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:1:# LLP 3000.015: The shared baseline for Snapback 5 — field-level write authority, engine sequences, row-keyed maintained views
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:8:**Revised:** 2026-10-15 (§2: digest identity adoption re-keys retained sequence counters and preserves their high-water values); 2026-10-14 (§3: PAGE4 request-meter fence forwards structural types without inspection); 2026-10-13 (FLAKE: deterministic buffered-writer shutdown witness, §2.1); 2026-10-03 (§3: receipt-derived prediction follows LLP 3000.022 §14.3); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 9 preserves maintenance types across job viewer scopes and embedded queries); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 8 makes maintenance types scope-wide across all assignments and helper calls); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 7 default-denies inspecting uses of nested maintenance-bearing values); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 6 replaces flat maintenance provenance with a static receiver-type and returned-data allowlist); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 4 makes cache invalidation private and certifies every computed predicate field); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 3 joins member/source lifetime dispatch, repairs lapsed-member restoration and rejects malformed evidence); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 2 restricts numerical reuse to non-expiring recipients, restarts and versions recount prefixes on rollback, and admits against current heap occupancy); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG round 1 incremental certification); 2026-10-02 (§3: BG bounds live stream certification scans with the existing decoder-page admission); 2026-09-30 (§2.1: counted console-line framing beside shutdown diagnostics, Lane SL); 2026-09-29 (§3: HV round 3 restores private witness scratch in single-candidate acquisition admission); 2026-09-29 (§3: HV round 2 admits decoder pages against the saved caller ledger); 2026-09-29 (§3: HV bounded cold-acquisition live-basis scans); 2026-09-29 (§2.1: DX quiet empty reads, opt-in evidence, counted console windows and acknowledged shutdown); 2026-09-28 (MP round 3: asset/job refusal coverage and supervisor visibility-filtering scope ruling, §2.1); 2026-09-28 (MP round 2: response-complete dev evidence, reader-owned sites and capture phase, §2.1); 2026-09-28 (RG round 1: charge abandoned probes against the fallback recount allowance; bounded indexed receipt suffix certification); 2026-09-28 (Lane MP round 1: same-group sequence proof, scoped asynchronous dev evidence and capture-denial causes, §2.1); 2026-09-28 (Lane MP: first-member sequence guidance and dev-only refusing clauses, §2.1); 2026-09-27 (FA round 3: reconcile the Following launch answer alongside receipt gaps); 2026-09-27 (ER round 4: initial expiry-scan receipt recipe and probe fallback); 2026-09-27 (data-modeling review: coordinate-bound probe guidance, caller authority and receipt resumption); 2026-09-27 (calibration 23: receipt-gap authoring recipe and bounded-probe diagnostic); 2026-09-26 (P10: adoption stays atomic while detached readers use the previous generation; LLP 3000.019 §3.6); 2026-09-25 (fix-forward: effective inherited row-view lifetimes and complete witness deadlines); 2026-09-25 (merged with 0.4.2 under one law: 0.4.2's read-through, event-slot and recount reads draw from §2's allowance; deadlines ride 0.4.2's scoped `Expiry`; a device point read that cannot examine a horizon-bounded card is withheld; the page reservation applies to pages over inherited aggregates); 2026-09-25 (lifetime-independent compound branch admission and no internal counter export); 2026-09-25 (integrated landing corrections: bounded witnesses, public admission, device deferral and private maintenance yields); 2026-09-25 (B3 live-basis row views integrated with B1 and B2); 2026-09-25 (B1 sealed disclosure integrated over B2 whole-rule authorization); 2026-09-25 (whole-rule expiry timelines and one request-wide physical authorization allowance); 2026-09-25 (bounded expiring cap scans, successful-predicate expiry dependencies, raw-storage token fence, exact walk boundary); 2026-09-25 (author restores bounded walks; every effect prepays whole rules; required authorization token); 2026-09-25 (one prepaid authorization scan, compound rules, cap and payload matrix, JSON counter descriptions); 2026-09-24 (initial)

llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:808:| Scalar point/page reads, prepaid point reads and numeric witnesses | Both `live_aggregate` entry points dispatch row-keyed views before the ordinary aggregate's source-only fast path. `live_row_view_inner` authorizes the current member and source group, then uses the joint lifetime classification. Expiring members always miss numerical reuse and take the authorized walk. Witness predicates on `value` use this same function; incomplete values cannot decide a writer/rule. Maintenance predicates refuse at admission. Pure existence and copied-field witnesses reveal no numerical count. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:809:| Batch reads and stream preparation | `live_batch` and `delivery_counts` use the same joint classification. Certificate reuse still refuses every expiring member; the full group scan uses the parent index for non-expiring sources, or the null/future expiry ranges for expiring sources, with the same caller-reserved page admission. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:813:| Member lifetime extension/restoration and adoption | `row_view_member` uses the joint classification for its no-change shortcut, interval delta selection and bounded recount. A lapsed member restored at a later clock therefore recounts, or leaves private pending work; reads still walk independently. An ordinary mutation cannot address an already-expired member (`NOT_FOUND`); seed/import restoration replaces the expired predecessor and already passes on main. A separate old/new-image maintenance regression at 1, 11 and 12 demonstrates the unsafe helper shortcut on main and its repair here; it is not claimed as an ordinary mutation reproduction. Immutable read-through parents cannot be edited in place; adoption rebuilds for changed lifetime/default/predicate semantics and recaptures inherited inputs. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:814:| Server source-event maintenance | An expiring recipient's count and partial prefix may both have missed earlier deltas. A current source event restarts private work while preserving cached fields; it never subtracts from that stale cache or writes a placeholder value/pending flag. This prevents an ordinary delete after rollback from underflowing zero and refusing with `E_OVERFLOW`. The write still emits its usual view refresh, whose delivery certifies current input. No source-group walk is added to the writer, and non-expiring recipients retain incremental deltas. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:815:| Background recount and the remaining stored-count path | Recount completion is private whenever either table can expire, and cannot publish a skipped cache as a new authoritative result. Only a source/member pair with neither effective expiry declaration returns its maintained stored value directly. Every such recipient receives source events transactionally; member predicate/parent changes rebuild or leave private work, projected as pending only at read time. The remaining source-only checks select expiry indexes, source-basis subtraction and ordinary (non-row-keyed) aggregates; none returns a row-keyed value around the joint guard. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:822:a source event for an expiring member restarts private work. Existing numerical
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:833:| `recountPending` | Exact **this view read's count incompleteness** as result data, never the private worker's status. It may be selected only directly into a query's returned shape. Every other read refuses at admission with `E_VIEW_KEY`, naming the field and the rewrite to `value` or member fields. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:834:| `pending` | The public prediction marker belongs to the predicted view row. Live reconstruction preserves it on Memory, SQLite and the tested wasm device; it is not copied from the member. It is the only additional row metadata in the prediction contract. Full-row results retain it; direct query projections may select it (optional when no prediction marker is present). Every non-output access refuses. |
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:835:| Other undeclared fields and private `$basis`/`$recount` columns | No public data projection or predicate access. Refuse rather than interpreting absent bookkeeping as null. The field walk covers all reachable names, not just declared view columns. |

llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:839:For live-basis views, running a private chunk at the same clock with unchanged
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:847:`last`, `at`, `slice` and list concatenation. Round 7 refuses sort/group on
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:872:conservatively refused at inspecting consumers; the diagnostic says **"use a
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:884:like a helper: selecting maintenance state there refuses even when the same
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:895:All other selections refuse, including discarded bindings, a status alias later
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:920:or inequality is a non-inspecting presence test; ordering against null is refused.
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:930:private `$basis`/`$recount` state: `id`, `cursor`, `total`, `parked`, `at`,
llp/3000.015-shared-baseline-for-snapback5.spec.md:932:on a view and all other undeclared names (including `$private`) refuse even in

# Client: answers stay on screen

**Start here:** `useQueryData`/`useQueryResult` ([short screens](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#short-screens)); metadata: `useQuery`/`usePage`; `useFollowing` (`guide feeds`); lists: `ReadState`.

```tsx
import { matchRead, readPresentation, usePage } from "snapback4/react";
import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
export function Inbox() { const page = usePage(api.inboxCards, {}), status = readPresentation(page.read); return <>{matchRead(page.read, { loading: () => "Loading…", denied: why => why.message,
    absent: () => "No conversations", partial: () => "Partial results", present: rows => `${rows.length} conversations` })}
    {status.incomplete && "Results may be incomplete"}{page.items.map(row => <p key={row.id}>{row.title} ({row.memberCount})</p>)}<button disabled={!page.hasMore} onClick={page.loadMore}>More</button></>;
}
```

- `E_OFFLINE`: unavailable; reconnect.
- `E_QUERY_RETRY`: exhausted; retry.

`usePage` exposes `items`, `hasMore`, `loadMore`, `read`, `loading` and `why`.
`loading` is true only for a loading combined read; `why` is its refusal or null.
Empty, partial and stale data are data: use `page.read` for completeness and
freshness. `client.observe(op, args)` returns an observation: call
`observation.subscribe(listener)` and read `observation.getSnapshot()`; close it
on cleanup. Asset display uses `useAsset(id)`, whose result has `status` and,
when ready, `url`; uploading uses `client.upload(bytes)` or `useUpload`.

## Reference

Proofs/exports use `exhaustPages` from `snapback4/proof`; screens use `usePage`.
- Treat the cursor as opaque: pass each reply's `next` back unchanged.
- Projection changes row fields only, never page order, completeness or cursor meaning.
- Every accepted page says `complete:true`, `cut:null` and nonpartial existence. The helper refuses denied, loading, malformed, repeated, capped, incomplete, partial or over-limit pages rather than returning a partial snapshot.
`Write`: pending custody, fresh sent result, replay without result, or failed. Clear on sentResult; undefined is not success.
```tsx
import { useState } from "react"; import { matchRead, sentResult, useAppReady, useMutation, useQuery } from "snapback4/react";
import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
export function Composer({conversationId}:{conversationId:string}) {
  const [draft,setDraft]=useState(""),[submitted,setSubmitted]=useState("");
  const ready=useAppReady(),send=useMutation(api.send),member=useQuery(api.myMembership,{conversationId});
  const blocked=matchRead(member,{denied:()=>true,absent:read=>read.fresh&&!read.revalidating,partial:()=>false,otherwise:()=>false});
  async function submit(){const text=draft.trim();if(!ready||blocked||!text||text.length>2000)return;setSubmitted(text);const write=await send.run({conversationId,body:text});if(sentResult(write)!==undefined)setDraft(current=>current.trim()===text?"":current);}
  return <><input value={draft} onChange={e=>setDraft(e.target.value)}/>
    <button disabled={!ready||send.inFlight||blocked} onClick={()=>void submit()}>Send</button>
    {send.last?.state==="failed"&&submitted&&<p role="alert">Not sent: {submitted} <button onClick={()=>setDraft(current=>current?current+" "+submitted:submitted)}>Edit</button> <button onClick={()=>setSubmitted("")}>Dismiss</button></p>}</>;
}
```
Membership has no horizon: the conversation's own group and messages' audience prove existence unless a pending prediction blocks absence. `loading` means acquisition or later withdrawal. Fresh, non-revalidating `existence(member) === "absent"` blocks; denied blocks. Never add `with own keys` to admin-written memberships. `waitSeconds:0` returns custody; retain its id, never auto-resend. Drafts/receipts: [reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md).

## An online role form across releases
Own createApp({url,writes:"online",sessionStore:localStorageSessionStore()}) outside render under AppProvider.
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"; import { isPresent, matchReadList, sentResult, useAppReady, useMutation, useQuery, useRelease } from "snapback4/react"; import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
export function StaffWorkspace({team}: {team: string}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(""), [selected, setSelected] = useState("");
  const ready = useAppReady(), release = useRelease(), role = useQuery(api.myRole, {team});
  const [lastStaff, setLastStaff] = useState(false);
  const staff = api.myRole.available(release) && isPresent(role) && role.complete && role.fresh && !role.revalidating && role.data.role === "staff", uncertain = "loading" in role || ("data" in role && (role.existence === "partial" || !role.complete || !role.fresh || role.revalidating === true));
  useEffect(() => { if (!uncertain) setLastStaff(staff); }, [uncertain, staff]);
  const shell = uncertain ? lastStaff : staff;
  const available = api.addStaffNote.available(release) && api.staffNotes.available(release), add = useMutation(api.addStaffNote);
  async function submit() {const title = draft.trim(); if (!ready || !staff || !available || !title || !selected) return;
    const write = await add.run({team, title, category:selected}); if (sentResult(write) !== undefined) setDraft(current => current === draft ? "" : current);}
  return <section aria-label="Staff workspace"><p>{shell ? "Staff workspace" : "Staff access required"}</p>
    <label>Note<input value={draft} onChange={e => setDraft(e.target.value)} /></label>
    <label htmlFor="category">Category</label><select id="category" value={selected} onChange={e => setSelected(e.target.value)}><option value="">Choose</option><option value="general">General</option></select>
    <button disabled={!ready || !staff || !available || add.inFlight} onClick={() => void submit()}>Save note</button>
    {staff && available ? <StaffRows team={team} /> : <p>{"denied" in role || "denied" in release || ("generation" in release && !available) || (!uncertain && !staff) ? "Staff workspace not available" : "Waiting for staff capabilities"}</p>}
    {add.last?.state === "failed" && <p role="alert">{add.last.why.message}</p>}
  </section>;
}
function StaffRows({team}: {team:string}) {
  const rows = useQuery(api.staffNotes, {team}); return <>{matchReadList(rows, {denied: why => <p role="alert">{why.message}</p>, absent: () => <p>No notes</p>,
    present: notes => <ul>{notes.map(row => <li key={row.id}>{row.title} — {row.priority ?? "normal"}</li>)}</ul>,
    otherwise: () => <p>Updating notes…</p>})}</>;
}
```
Mount `<StaffWorkspace key={team} team={team}/>` to preserve drafts. Gate reads/Save on settled role/operation availability; rules decide authority. Use fallbacks such as `row.priority ?? "normal"`.

## A public guest page

Guests (`use identity`/`use identity guests`): public reads require browser session custody.
```tsx
import { createApp } from "snapback4/app";
import { localStorageSessionStore } from "snapback4/auth";
import { AppProvider, matchReadList, useAuth, useQuery } from "snapback4/react";
import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
const guestApp = createApp({url:"http://127.0.0.1:4400", readOnly:true, sessionStore:localStorageSessionStore()});
export function GuestPage() { return <AppProvider app={guestApp}><GuestGate /></AppProvider>; }
function GuestGate() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  if (auth.state === "restoring") return <p>Restoring…</p>;
  if (auth.state === "signed-out") return <><button onClick={() => void auth.guest()}>Continue as a guest</button><p role="alert">{auth.why?.message}</p></>;
  return <PublicNotes />;
}
function PublicNotes() {
  const notes = useQuery(api.publicNotes, {});
  return <>{matchReadList(notes, {denied: why => <p role="alert">{why.message}</p>, absent: () => <p>No public notes</p>,
    present: rows => <ul>{rows.map(row => <li key={row.id}>{row.title}</li>)}</ul>, otherwise: () => <p>Loading…</p>})}</>;
}
```

## React hooks and auth decisions

Never gate sign-in with `useAppReady()`: it needs a signed-in client; sign-out/replacement resets it. Keep drafts above gates; readiness is not authority. Invalid online/device/readOnly combinations set `useAuth().why` and fail app.ready; recreate corrected. Storage failures throw `E_ADMISSION`.
`useAuth()`: `state`/`viewer`/`why`, `signup`/`login`/`guest`/`logout`/`logoutEverywhere`; client `signOut()`; Expo secureStoreSessionStore(SecureStore). Password/custody: [reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#password-react-and-expo). E_SESSION names invalid fields. Online tokens return no session and refresh explicitly; automatic refresh/devices need complete Sessions.

| Helper | Input/result | Handlers/arguments |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `matchRead(read, handlers)` | Read or page; handler result | `loading(read)`, `denied(why,read)`, `absent(read)`, `partial(data,read)`, `present(data,read)`. Bare `why: Refusal`; data first, metadata second. All five required, even partial on single rows; `otherwise(read)` replaces omitted loading/absent/present only. |
| `matchReadList(read, handlers)` | List read or page | Requires `denied(why,read)` and `otherwise(read)`; omitted partial uses otherwise. |
| `otherwiseAll(fallback, overrides?)` | Spread into matchRead handlers | `fallback(read)` includes denied/partial; named handlers win in any order. |
| `usePage(op, args)` | Query with `c: cursor ?`; omit c from args | `{read, items, hasMore, loadMore}`; match page.read/page, render items, call loadMore(). No handlers. |

`'data' in read` exposes data/existence/metadata. Complete empty lists are absent. `isPresent` excludes null; `isReady` permits it; `existence(read)` and `read.existence` classify. `absent` can be an authorised empty list. PDFs need `doc: file <=10000000 ?`; persist uploaded ids through mutations (`guide assets`).
```ts
import type { Read, Client } from "snapback4/contract";
export function listLabel(read: Read<readonly {id:string}[]>): string {
  if ('denied' in read) return "not available"; if ('data' in read) return read.existence === "absent" ? "No rows" : `${read.data.length} rows`; return "Loading…"; }
export async function uploadPdf(client: Client, picked: File) { return client.upload(picked, {file: {type: "application/pdf", name: picked.name}}); }
```
`loading`: keep shells; `denied`: show refusal; `present`: render; `absent`: proven empty; `partial`: uncertain. Offline absent is absence at the acquired watermark. Hidden/missing rows are indistinguishable. `readPresentation` notices never grant permission. Unknown counts never hide conversations. `predicts`: eligible/device/held facts may render `pending:true` before `useMutation().run()` settles; others queue without rows. Never/online follows settlement/delivery. run() waits for sent/failed; `{waitSeconds:0}` returns custody. Proofs await fresh sent; predictions aren't settlement.
`query(op,args)` settles ready/denied within 30 seconds. Unavailable offline reads give immediate `E_OFFLINE`. After four rounds, exhaustion refuses retryable `E_QUERY_RETRY`; permanently unavailable held reads refuse non-retryable `E_QUERY_HELD`. Retention, RowOf/ArgsOf/ResultOf and {skip}: [reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md). Share/paginate: eight active, eight queued observation attempts; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths.
Browser: no sync → journal; sync → webDevice(); online writes: writes:"online". Native: nativeDevice. Every milestone: npm run export:ios; check proof in the background with witness status --wait. Rebuild: npm run native:smoke. Reopen/reconnect/write replay: guide witness. useOnline: server reads/ephemeral/actions; app.mutate: durable writes. 2. **`sequence by <ref>`** on a column: an engine-assigned, dense,
   per-parent integer, assigned at commit, provisional under prediction.
3. **A maintained view keyed by another table's row:** `view name: count T

   reads changes by more than the meter allows in the writer's transaction.

Together they let Clique's read marks, unread counts and message ordering

## 1. `written by`

```quarry
table members:
  conversationId: conversations
  principal: principal
  readUpTo: int default 0 written by .principal
  read <- exists members[.conversationId, viewer]
  update (old, next) <- exists members[conversationId = old.conversationId, principal = viewer, role = 'admin']
                     and next.readUpTo = old.readUpTo
```

- **Authority.** The value of a `written by .p` column may be written only
  by the principal named in column `p` of the same row. An insert sets it
  to its declared default; a supplied value is refused with
  `E_FIELD_AUTHORITY` unless the inserter is that principal. A patch that
  touches the column is authorized for that column by that principal, and
  for every other field by the row's `update` rule. The owner may patch the
  column alone with no row `update` rule; the row's `update (old, next)`
  rule may still constrain the transition (monotonic marks), as above.
- **Projection.** By default the column is **private to its owner**: it
  never appears in a log image, a query row, a shape, a cursor or a write
  result delivered to any principal other than the owner. The owner sees
  it on every device. `readable by audience` on the column makes it an
  ordinary column for reads. The implementation must deliver the private
  value to the owner's devices through the owner's own partition, not by
  filtering the group image per reader, because group images are shared
  by audience.
- **Caller accounting law.** The caller-visible meter charges exactly two
  kinds of quantity, subject to the paging residual below: fixed reservations derived from declared bounds (column
  bounds, index arity, page size, and the rule's witness count), and the
  disclosed result after row-read and field authorization both succeed.
  Candidate decoding, rule evaluation, witness probes, and materialization
  of unreadable rows run on an internal ledger under that reservation; no
  actual charge from that ledger enters the caller's receipt or cut.
  `authorized_scan` is the shared B1/B2 boundary: admission precedes candidate
  access, and every return restores the caller's ledger, ceilings and cut
  policy. B1 adds transient declared decoder/witness scratch around this
  boundary for point reads, scan candidates and write-target resolution.
  Candidate materialization, expiry filtering, row authorization and private
  projection finish inside that scope; only surviving `AuthorizedRow` images
  can be charged afterward. `Disclosed` alone has no pricing authority.
  Rule witnesses export verdict/coverage and the complete-rule deadline
  defined in §2 (including simultaneous and correlated alternatives). Public, non-expiring tables with
  no private fields or recorded captures prove row authority before access and may use actual-size
  decoder admission for reads, fetches, lookups and writes, including a short
  value under a large declaration. Their patch copies price the sealed public
  image, without reserving the declaration's maximum payload. System maintenance owns its own ledger,
  and device physical authority work retains its separate uncertainty budget.
- **Observable noninterference.** Subject to the three residuals stated in
  §1–§3, private input changes must leave an
  unauthorized principal's results, refusal envelope, meter, changes table
  set, sync images/watermarks and maintenance sequence unchanged. Projection
  copies only disclosed fields and missing ordinary-field defaults; it never
  reinstates an omitted private default. Private fields cannot supply public
  derived values, including Following mappings and predicates under either
  spelling. Non-uniform server scans of a table with private fields require
  a readable audience scope in B1; B2's authorized scan supplies the shared
  page-reservation implementation for the combined baseline.
  Hidden delete and absent upsert field-authority refusals use the ordinary
  `NOT_FOUND` attempt boundary. Direct inserts and readable write targets
  keep their detailed errors. A named transition cannot test another owner's
  private value to decide an ordinary patch: require the field's owner, except
  for value-independent comparisons of an untouched field with itself. A patch
  retaining an unreadable row's fields or a non-owned private payload charges
  declared column copy bounds, never hidden lengths. Ordered self-comparisons of nullable private fields
  are value-dependent; equality/inequality and required numeric
  self-comparisons can be folded before evaluating omitted device values.
  Decoder reservations cover UTF-8/escaping, map and array growth, recursive
  list/JSON bounds, bounded asset facts and the 144-byte identity admission
  bound. SQLite lends its stored JSON during decode. A large declaration can
  exceed the heap even for an empty value; both worlds then refuse identically.
  No runtime ceiling is raised. The raw retained-row `Value::heap_bytes` API
  and `Context`-minted system token are removed. The system projection token has a private
  runner-only constructor; PostgreSQL raw cache pricing charges an opaque
  internal ledger that exports no price. Raw scalar and serialized-storage
  prices are opaque `InternalPrice` values, chargeable only to an opaque
  `InternalMeter` handle lent by `InternalLedger`; retaining a caller's `Meter`
  inside that closure cannot redirect the charge. The handle exposes neither
  `Deref` nor counters, and consuming the ledger cannot return its meter.
  Metered adapters require the handle and authorization scope too. Exact retained-image
  pricing requires sealed row-read evidence, and expression pricing keeps its
  private evaluator capability. Supplied write values carry sealed input
  provenance through defaults, key completion and immutable-field removal;
  retained old fields use declared copy bounds. Authorized result keys and
  evaluated cursor/input keys retain their separate evidence. There is no
  writer helper that accepts a raw old value and returns a caller price.
  Compile-fail tests cover raw materialization, fabricated system/row authority,
  unproven field projection, raw owned scalar charging with and without a scope,
  and an unscoped metered storage call.
  Owner delivery prepays one step and the rule's static witness count per
  declared private-field slot and row image; exact recipient checks use an
  internal ledger. Mutation receipts count private-table events by table and

  Wildcard shapes select private fields through the same optional access as
